# Repository Map

Generated by `_tools/workspace-index/src/workspace_index.py`.

## Root Folders

| Path | Class | Purpose | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `.agents/` | runtime_adapter | Google Antigravity workspace rule adapters that point to shared operating principles. | _ops/projects/root-structure-policy.json |
| `.claude/` | runtime_adapter | Claude Code project rule adapters that point to shared operating principles. | _ops/projects/root-structure-policy.json |
| `.cursor/` | runtime_adapter | Cursor project rule adapters that point to shared operating principles. | _ops/projects/root-structure-policy.json |
| `_archive/` | reserved_operational | Paused or retired tracked projects. | _ops/projects/root-structure-policy.json |
| `_docs/` | reserved_operational | Workspace-level documentation, policies, and decision records. | _ops/projects/root-structure-policy.json |
| `_history/` | reserved_operational | Dated work history, request summaries, traces, evaluations, plans, installations, and context archives. | _ops/projects/root-structure-policy.json |
| `_ops/` | reserved_operational | Operations hub, prompts, workflows, coordination, maps, project registry, and install registry. | _ops/projects/root-structure-policy.json |
| `_philosophy/` | reserved_operational | Foundational agent and platform operating philosophy. | _ops/projects/root-structure-policy.json |
| `_private/` | local_only | Local private notes or scratch state that must not be committed or used as durable knowledge. | _ops/projects/root-structure-policy.json |
| `_requirements/` | reserved_operational | Shared requirements baselines, changes, and reviews. | _ops/projects/root-structure-policy.json |
| `_research/` | reserved_operational | Reusable internet research and external reference notes. | _ops/projects/root-structure-policy.json |
| `_skills/` | reserved_operational | Tracked source copies of custom Codex skills. | _ops/projects/root-structure-policy.json |
| `_specs/` | reserved_operational | Shared spec-driven artifacts, plans, tasks, validation, and traceability. | _ops/projects/root-structure-policy.json |
| `_templates/` | reserved_operational | Reusable project, document, research, and artifact templates. | _ops/projects/root-structure-policy.json |
| `_tools/` | reserved_operational | Reusable deterministic local tools and scripts. | _ops/projects/root-structure-policy.json |
| `agent-platform/` | registered_project | Central Python-first platform project for building, configuring, evaluating, and improving agents. | _ops/projects/registry.json |
| `design-asset-library/` | registered_project | Reusable, legally cautious SVG design asset library for presentation decks, HTML artifacts, dashboards, prototypes, and platform visual systems. | _ops/projects/registry.json |
| `outputs/` | local_only | Transient local output from one-off tool runs; durable artifacts must move into the owning project artifacts folder. | _ops/projects/root-structure-policy.json |
| `platform-desktop-app/` | registered_project | Desktop/installable software productization project for turning the workspace platform into an end-user application with OS-specific installer, release, privacy, and update gates. | _ops/projects/registry.json |
| `presentation-agent/` | registered_project | Presentation-focused agent project for collecting presentation design references, PPT/HTML sources, reusable assets, and script-flow evidence. | _ops/projects/registry.json |
| `workspace-monitor/` | registered_project | Vercel-deployable Next.js monitoring site that visualizes repository history, projects, agents, requirements, specs, evaluations, and Markdown documents from a generated workspace snapshot. | _ops/projects/registry.json |

## Logical Architecture Layers

| Layer | Purpose | Folders | Rule |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `project-plane` | Independently owned root projects with their own lifecycle, commands, README, docs, tests, and artifacts. | `agent-platform/`, `presentation-agent/`, `platform-desktop-app/`, `workspace-monitor/` | Add a new root project only when the concern has independent ownership or lifecycle; register it in _ops/projects/registry.json. |
| `operations-control-plane` | Shared execution control, policies, requirements, specs, history, coordination, workflows, prompts, maps, and evaluations. | `_ops/`, `_docs/`, `_requirements/`, `_specs/`, `_history/` | These folders are shared platform control surfaces, not project-specific storage. |
| `knowledge-and-reuse-plane` | Reusable knowledge, philosophy, templates, tools, skills, research, and archived projects. | `_philosophy/`, `_research/`, `_skills/`, `_templates/`, `_tools/`, `_archive/` | Promote project-local assets here only when they are reusable across projects. |
| `runtime-adapter-plane` | Thin adapters for specific AI coding tools that point back to shared operating principles. | `.claude/`, `.cursor/`, `.agents/` | Do not fork policy here; keep adapters thin. |
| `protected-local-plane` | Local-only sensitive or private material that exists on disk but is not durable repository knowledge. | `_private/` | Do not read, index, summarize, or use contents by default. Route real sensitive files to _private/sensitive/ or an external secret manager. |
| `generated-local-plane` | Transient local output that is not a durable artifact until moved into the owning project artifacts folder. | `outputs/` | Do not treat root outputs as final deliverables. |

## Key Start Points

- `_ops/index.md`: operations navigation
- `_ops/prompts/00-router.md`: prompt selection
- `_ops/workflows/00-start-here.md`: default work sequence
- `_docs/instructions/persistent-instructions.md`: durable user instructions
- `agent-platform/README.md`: platform project overview
